This entanglement may make adapter composition less reliable: when multiple adapters are merged at inference time, their overlapping task behaviors can accumulate together with document-specific updates, potentially making the merged adapter less stable and less focused on the intended document knowledge. To examine this issue, we explore Orthogonal Subspace Decomposition (OSD), an adapter-training setup that separates reusable task behavior from document-specific knowledge adapters. Concretely, we first train a Task LoRA to capture reusable task behavior, and then train document LoRAs to encode document-specific knowledge in a orthogonal subspace. This setup provides a controlled way to examine how orthogonalizing task and document LoRA updates affects adapter composition in multi-document PRAG. Experiments across multiple knowledge-intensive tasks and model scales suggest that this orthogonalization strategy can improve compositional robustness in parametric RAG, especially when multiple document adapters are merged.

</details>


### [116] [HalluCiteChecker: A Lightweight Toolkit for Hallucinated Citation Detection and Verification in the Era of AI Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26835)
*Yusuke Sakai,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了HalluCiteChecker，一个用于检测和验证科学论文中幻觉引用的工具包，旨在减轻审稿人负担并支持出版前的系统性检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI助手在学术写作中的应用虽然提高了效率，但也导致了不存在的“幻觉引用”的出现，损害了论文的可信度，并增加了审稿人和作者的核实负担。

Method: 将幻觉引用检测形式化为自然语言处理任务，并开发了一个轻量级、可在普通笔记本电脑上快速运行的工具包，支持离线操作且仅需CPU即可高效执行。

Result: HalluCiteChecker能够在几秒内完成引用验证，完全离线运行，无需依赖外部服务器或GPU资源。

Conclusion: 该工具包有助于减少审稿工作量，支持会议和期刊在审稿前进行系统的引用检查，代码已开源并可通过PyPI安装。

Abstract: We introduce HalluCiteChecker, a toolkit for detecting and verifying hallucinated citations in scientific papers. While AI assistant technologies have transformed the academic writing process, including citation recommendation, they have also led to the emergence of hallucinated citations that do not correspond to any existing work. Such citations not only undermine the credibility of scientific papers but also impose an additional burden on reviewers and authors, who must manually verify their validity during the review process. In this study, we formalize hallucinated citation detection as an NLP task and provide a corresponding toolkit as a practical foundation for addressing this problem. Our package is lightweight and can perform verification in seconds on a standard laptop. It can also be executed entirely offline and runs efficiently using only CPUs. We hope that HalluCiteChecker will help reduce reviewer workload and support organizers by enabling systematic pre-review and publication checks. Our code is released under the Apache 2.0 license on GitHub and is distributed as an installable package via PyPI. A demonstration video is available on YouTube.

</details>


### [117] [What Kind of Language is Easy to Language-Model Under Curriculum Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26844)
*Nadine El-Naggar,Tatsuki Kuribayashi,Ted Briscoe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了课程学习（CL）对语言模型归纳偏见的影响，发现发展性动机的CL显著改变了语言模型在类型学特征组合上的预测表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言模型的学习偏见是否足以解释语言类型学中的普遍模式，并引入学习场景（如课程学习）作为新的分析维度。

Method: 基于现有语言模型的研究，引入一种简单的课程学习变体，从简单句子开始逐步训练模型，并与随机输入顺序进行对比。

Result: 课程学习显著影响了语言模型表现出的归纳偏见，表明学习场景与模型偏见之间存在重要交互作用。

Conclusion: 学习策略（如课程学习）会显著改变语言模型的归纳偏见，提示在模拟语言类型学时需考虑学习过程的设计。

Abstract: Many of the thousands of attested languages share common configurations of features, creating a spectrum from typologically very rare (e.g., object-verb-subject word order) or impossible languages to very common combinations of features (e.g., subject-object-verb word order). One central question is under what conditions such typological tendencies can be predicted, and specifically whether the learning bias of language models (LMs) is sufficient to reproduce such patterns. In this study, we add one dimensionality to such analysis -- the learning scenario for LMs -- to explore its interaction with the inductive bias of LMs. Specifically, as a first study, we examine the effect of curriculum learning (CL), as a developmentally motivated learning scenario, i.e., starting with simpler sentences rather than randomly-ordered input. We expand existing LM-based exploration (El-Naggar et al., 2025a,b) with a simple CL variant and find that CL substantially impacts the apparent inductive bias of LMs.

</details>


### [118] [MoRFI: Monotonic Sparse Autoencoder Feature Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26866)
*Dimitris Dimakopoulos,Shay B. Cohen,Ioannis Konstas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过控制微调实验，发现引入新知识会增加大语言模型的幻觉现象，并利用稀疏自编码器和提出的MoRFI方法识别出导致幻觉的残差流中的潜在方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解监督微调过程中新知识引入导致大语言模型产生幻觉的机制。

Method: 在Llama、Gemma和Mistral等模型上进行封闭式问答的控制微调实验，使用预训练的稀疏自编码器分析残差流激活，并提出MoRFI方法识别与幻觉相关的单调响应特征。

Result: 发现逐步引入新知识会增加幻觉，长时间训练更明显；MoRFI能跨模型一致地识别出影响知识检索的潜在方向。

Conclusion: 新知识微调会干扰模型原有知识检索，存在可识别的因果性潜在方向导致幻觉，单个潜在干预可恢复知识。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) acquire most of their factual knowledge during the pre-training stage, through next token prediction. Subsequent stages of post-training often introduce new facts outwith the parametric knowledge, giving rise to hallucinations. While it has been demonstrated that supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on new knowledge may exacerbate the problem, the underlying mechanisms are still poorly understood. We conduct a controlled fine-tuning experiment, focusing on closed-book QA, and find latent directions that causally contribute to hallucinations. Specifically, we fine-tune Llama 3.1 8B, Gemma 2 9B and Mistral 7B v03 on seven distinct single QA datasets, controlling for the percentage of new knowledge and number of training epochs. By measuring performance on the test set, we validate that incrementally introducing new knowledge increases hallucinations, with the effect being more pronounced with prolonged training. We leverage pre-trained sparse autoencoders (SAEs) to analyze residual stream activations across various checkpoints for each model and propose Monotonic Relationship Feature Identification (MoRFI) for capturing causally relevant latents. MoRFI filters SAE features that respond monotonically to controlled fine-tuning data mixtures of a target property. Our findings show that exposure to unknown facts disrupts the model's ability to retrieve stored knowledge along a set of directions in the residual stream. Our pipeline reliably discovers them across distinct models, recovering knowledge through single-latent interventions.

</details>


### [119] [HealthNLP_Retrievers at ArchEHR-QA 2026: Cascaded LLM Pipeline for Grounded Clinical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26880)
*Md Biplob Hosen,Md Alomgeer Hussein,Md Akmol Masud,Omar Faruque,Tera L Reynolds,Lujie Karen Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种基于Gemini 2.5 Pro大语言模型的多阶段级联管道系统，用于在电子健康记录（EHR）上实现 grounded 问答，以帮助患者更好地理解和使用其医疗信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管患者门户提供了对电子健康记录的直接访问，但复杂的临床信息仍难以被患者理解与利用，因此需要一种能准确解释患者问题并从临床笔记中提取相关证据的问答系统。

Method: 采用四模块级联架构：（1）少样本查询重构单元；（2）基于启发式的证据打分器；（3）基于证据的回答生成器；（4）高精度的多对多答案-证据对齐框架，并基于Gemini 2.5 Pro模型实现。

Result: 该系统在ArchEHR-QA 2026任务中表现优异，在问题理解子任务中排名第1，回答生成第5，证据识别第7，答案-证据对齐第9。

Conclusion: 将大语言模型嵌入结构化多阶段流程可提升回答的准确性、专业性和证据依从性，有助于改善面向患者的医疗沟通质量。

Abstract: Patient portals now give individuals direct access to their electronic health records (EHRs), yet access alone does not ensure patients understand or act on the complex clinical information contained in these records. The ArchEHR-QA 2026 shared task addresses this challenge by focusing on grounded question answering over EHRs, and this paper presents the system developed by the HealthNLP_Retrievers team for this task. The proposed approach uses a multi-stage cascaded pipeline powered by the Gemini 2.5 Pro large language model to interpret patient-authored questions and retrieve relevant evidence from lengthy clinical notes. Our architecture comprises four integrated modules: (1) a few-shot query reformulation unit which summarizes verbose patient queries; (2) a heuristic-based evidence scorer which ranks clinical sentences to prioritize recall; (3) a grounded response generator which synthesizes professional-caliber answers restricted strictly to identified evidence; and (4) a high-precision many-to-many alignment framework which links generated answers to supporting clinical sentences. This cascaded approach achieved competitive results. Across the individual tracks, the system ranked 1st in question interpretation, 5th in answer generation, 7th in evidence identification, and 9th in answer-evidence alignment. These results show that integrating large language models within a structured multi-stage pipeline improves grounding, precision, and the professional quality of patient-oriented health communication. To support reproducibility, our source code is publicly available in our GitHub repository

</details>


### [120] [Select to Think: Unlocking SLM Potential with Local Sufficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26940)
*Wenxuan Ye,Yangyang Zhang,Xueli An,Georg Carle,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SELECT TO THINK (S2T) 方法，利用小语言模型（SLM）自身生成的 top-K 候选token中包含大模型（LLM）首选token的现象（局部充分性），将LLM的角色从生成转为选择，实现高效推理；进一步提出的 S2T-LOCAL 使SLM能在无LLM参与下自主重排序，显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型（SLM）在推理能力上弱于大模型（LLM），现有方法依赖LLM生成导致延迟和成本高，标准知识蒸馏又受限于SLM容量，难以模仿LLM复杂的生成分布。

Method: 发现局部充分性现象：在推理分歧点，LLM选择的token通常已存在于SLM的top-K预测中；基于此提出S2T框架，让LLM从生成改为对SLM的候选进行排序；进一步提出S2T-LOCAL，将这种选择逻辑通过蒸馏注入SLM，使其能自主重排序。

Result: 1.5B的SLM在其top-8预测中以95%命中率覆盖32B LLM的选择；S2T-LOCAL在多个基准上平均提升greedy decoding性能24.1%，效果媲美8路径self-consistency，但仅需单路径推理效率。

Conclusion: S2T及其本地化版本S2T-LOCAL有效弥合了小模型与大模型在推理能力上的差距，在保持低延迟和低成本的同时，显著提升了SLM的推理性能，为高效推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) offer computational efficiency for scalable deployment, yet they often fall short of the reasoning power exhibited by their larger counterparts (LLMs). To mitigate this gap, current approaches invoke an LLM to generate tokens at points of reasoning divergence, but these external calls introduce substantial latency and costs. Alternatively, standard distillation is often hindered by the capacity limitation, as SLMs struggle to accurately mimic the LLM's complex generative distribution. We address this dilemma by identifying local sufficiency: at divergence points, the LLM's preferred token consistently resides within the SLM's top-K next-token predictions, even when failing to emerge as the SLM top-1 choice. We therefore propose SELECT TO THINK (S2T), which reframes the LLM's role from open-ended generation to selection among the SLM's proposals, simplifying the supervision signal to discrete candidate rankings. Leveraging this, we introduce S2T-LOCAL, which distills the selection logic into the SLM, empowering it to perform autonomous re-ranking without inference-time LLM dependency. Empirically, we demonstrate that a 1.5B SLM's top-8 candidates capture the 32B LLM's choice with 95% hit rate. Translating this potential into performance, S2T-LOCAL improves greedy decoding by 24.1% on average across benchmarks, effectively matching the efficacy of 8-path self-consistency while operating with single-trajectory efficiency.

</details>


### [121] [Turning the TIDE: Cross-Architecture Distillation for Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26951)
*Gongbo Zhang,Wen Wang,Ye Tian,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了TIDE框架，首个支持跨架构扩散大语言模型（dLLM）知识蒸馏的方案，通过TIDAL、CompDemo和Reverse CALM三个模块，在不同架构、注意力机制和分词器间实现高效知识迁移，显著提升小规模学生模型在多项任务尤其是代码生成上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有dLLM蒸馏方法局限于单一架构内优化，无法实现跨架构知识转移，且对教师模型在不同噪声水平下的可靠性缺乏动态建模，限制了小型化dLLM的发展。

Method: 提出TIDE框架，包含：(1) TIDAL——动态调整训练进程与扩散时间步上的蒸馏强度；(2) CompDemo——通过互补掩码分割增强教师上下文；(3) Reverse CALM——跨分词器目标函数，反向匹配块级似然，实现梯度有界与双端噪声过滤。

Result: 将8B密集和16B MoE教师模型的知识蒸馏至0.6B学生模型，在八个基准上平均超越基线1.53分，HumanEval代码生成得分达48.78，显著优于AR基线的32.3。

Conclusion: TIDE首次实现了异构架构间的dLLM蒸馏，有效提升了小型模型性能，为高效部署高性能dLLM提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer parallel decoding and bidirectional context, but state-of-the-art dLLMs require billions of parameters for competitive performance. While existing distillation methods for dLLMs reduce inference steps within a single architecture, none address cross-architecture knowledge transfer, in which the teacher and student differ in architecture, attention mechanism, and tokenizer. We present TIDE, the first framework for cross-architecture dLLM distillation, comprising three modular components: (1) TIDAL, which jointly modulates distillation strength across training progress and diffusion timestep to account for the teacher's noise-dependent reliability; (2) CompDemo, which enriches the teacher's context via complementary mask splitting to improve predictions under heavy masking; and (3) Reverse CALM, a cross-tokenizer objective that inverts chunk-level likelihood matching, yielding bounded gradients and dual-end noise filtering. Distilling 8B dense and 16B MoE teachers into a 0.6B student via two heterogeneous pipelines outperforms the baseline by an average of 1.53 points across eight benchmarks, yielding notable gains in code generation, where HumanEval scores reach 48.78 compared to 32.3 for the AR baseline.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [Hierarchical Multi-Persona Induction from User Behavioral Logs: Learning Evidence-Grounded and Truthful Personas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26120)
*Nayoung Choi,Haeyu Jeong,Changbong Kim,Hongjun Lim,Jinho D. Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种层次化框架，通过聚合用户行为生成基于证据的多个人格，并利用优化方法提升人格质量和未来交互预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在从用户日志生成自然语言人格时，缺乏对人格本身质量的充分评估，且日志噪声大、意图交错。

Method: 将用户行为聚类为意图记忆，通过聚类和标注生成多个人格，将人格生成建模为包含连贯性、证据对齐和真实性等质量指标的优化问题，并采用分组直接偏好优化（DPO）训练模型。

Result: 在大规模服务日志和两个公开数据集上的实验表明，该方法生成的人格更连贯、更贴近证据、更可信，同时提升了未来交互预测性能。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效生成高质量、可解释且可信的用户人格，兼顾下游任务性能。

Abstract: Behavioral logs provide rich signals for user modeling, but are noisy and interleaved across diverse intents. Recent work uses LLMs to generate interpretable natural-language personas from user logs, yet evaluation often emphasizes downstream utility, providing limited assurance of persona quality itself. We propose a hierarchical framework that aggregates user actions into intent memories and induces multiple evidence-grounded personas by clustering and labeling these memories. We formulate persona induction as an optimization problem over persona quality-captured by cluster cohesion, persona-evidence alignment, and persona truthfulness-and train the persona model using a groupwise extension of Direct Preference Optimization (DPO). Experiments on a large-scale service log and two public datasets show that our method induces more coherent, evidence-grounded, and trustworthy personas, while also improving future interaction prediction.

</details>


### [123] [OMEGA: Optimizing Machine Learning by Evaluating Generated Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26211)
*Jeremy Nixon,Annika Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OMEGA是一个端到端框架，通过结构化元提示工程和可执行代码生成，自动化机器学习算法的研发，生成的算法在20个基准数据集上优于scikit-learn基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了加速和自动化AI研究，减少人工干预，提升机器学习算法开发效率。

Method: 结合结构化元提示工程与可执行代码生成技术，从想法生成到代码实现全自动创建新的机器学习分类器。

Result: 在20个基准数据集（infinity-bench）上，OMEGA生成的算法性能优于scikit-learn中的标准模型。

Conclusion: OMEGA框架能够有效自动生成高性能机器学习算法，推动AI研发的自动化进程。

Abstract: In order to automate AI research we introduce a full, end-to-end framework, OMEGA: Optimizing Machine learning by Evaluating Generated Algorithms, that starts at idea generation and ends with executable code. Our system combines structured meta-prompt engineering with executable code generation to create new ML classifiers. The OMEGA framework has been utilized to generate several novel algorithms that outperform scikit-learn baselines across a robust selection of 20 benchmark datasets (infinity-bench). You can access models discussed in this paper and more in the python package: pip install omega-models.

</details>


### [124] [Persuadability and LLMs as Legal Decision Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26233)
*Oisin Suttle,David Lillis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了前沿的开源和闭源大语言模型（LLMs）在法律决策中如何响应不同质量律师提出的论点，揭示其在面对高质量辩护时可能被过度说服的问题，从而对LLMs在司法和行政场景中的可行性应用提出质疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型被提议作为法律决策助手甚至初审裁决者，有必要探究它们如何回应对立双方提出的法律论点，以及哪些因素会影响其判决倾向，尤其是在复杂案件中保持中立与公正的能力。

Method: 通过原始实验设计，测试多个前沿大语言模型在面对相同法律问题但由不同质量律师陈述的论点时的反应，分析模型是否更倾向于支持表达更优或更具说服力的论点，并探究影响该倾向的关键因素。

Result: 实验结果显示，无论是开源还是闭源的大语言模型，均表现出对高质量辩护更强的认同倾向，即模型可能因论点表述的质量而非实质内容而改变判断，显示出被‘更好看的包装’所影响的风险。

Conclusion: 当前的大语言模型在法律决策支持应用中存在潜在偏倚风险，容易受辩护技巧影响而偏离案件实质，因此在司法和行政领域部署此类技术需谨慎，并应进一步改进模型的抗干扰能力和论证理解能力。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are proposed as legal decision assistants, and even first-instance decision-makers, across a range of judicial and administrative contexts, it becomes essential to explore how they answer legal questions, and in particular the factors that lead them to decide difficult questions in one way or another. A specific feature of legal decisions is the need to respond to arguments advanced by contending parties. A legal decision-maker must be able to engage with, and respond to, including through being potentially persuaded by, arguments advanced by the parties. Conversely, they should not be unduly persuadable, influenced by a particularly compelling advocate to decide cases based on the skills of the advocates, rather than the merits of the case. We explore how frontier open- and closed-weights LLMs respond to legal arguments, reporting original experimental results examining how the quality of the advocate making those arguments affects the likelihood that a model will agree with a particular legal point of view, and exploring the factors driving these results. Our results have implications for the feasibility of adopting LLMs across legal and administrative settings.

</details>


### [125] [Apriori-based Analysis of Learned Helplessness in Mathematics Tutoring: Behavioral Patterns by Level, Intervention, and Outcome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26237)
*John Paul P. Miranda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究利用Apriori算法分析数学辅导系统日志中与习得性无助（LH）相关的行为交互模式，发现不跳题与解题成功密切相关，而跳题尤其是不使用提示的跳题行为则显著关联于解题失败，尤其在高LH学生和有系统干预条件下更为明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同习得性无助水平及系统干预条件下学生在数学辅导系统中的行为模式如何影响问题解决结果，以揭示学习行为与心理状态之间的关联。

Method: 采用Apriori算法对数学辅导系统的交互日志数据进行关联规则分析，从习得性无助水平（高低）、系统干预（有无）和问题解决结果（解决/未解决）三个维度进行比较。

Result: 整体上，跳题且不使用提示是最常见的与未解决问题相关的模式；低LH学生表现出更强的坚持行为与解题成功的关联，以及使用提示与成功之间的正向关系；高LH学生更倾向于回避行为，跳题与未解决结果强相关；无干预组中坚持与成功的关系更强，而有干预组中跳题导致未解决的模式更显著；在所有群体中，不跳题始终与解题成功相关联。

Conclusion: 不跳题是预测问题解决成功的稳定行为指标，系统干预可能意外强化了跳题等回避行为，未来设计应更精细地引导学生使用提示并减少逃避策略，尤其针对高习得性无助学生。

Abstract: This study applied the Apriori algorithm to analyze behavioral interaction patterns associated with learned helplessness (LH) in mathematics tutoring system logs. Interaction data were examined across three dimensions: LH level (low vs. high), system-based intervention (with vs. without), and problem-solving outcomes (solved vs. unsolved). The analysis of the complete dataset showed that skipping problems without using hints was the most frequent pattern linked to unsolved outcomes, while persistence behaviors such as not skipping were less dominant overall. Comparisons by LH level showed that low-LH students had stronger links between problem solving and not skipping, as well as positive associations between hint use and solved outcomes. High-LH students showed more avoidance patterns, with skipping strongly tied to unsolved outcomes. In the comparison of system-based intervention conditions, students without intervention had the highest lift for persistence-success links, while the with-intervention group had stronger patterns involving skipping behaviors leading to unsolved outcomes. Outcome-specific analysis showed that not skipping was consistently associated with solved problems across all groups, while skipping without hints predicted unsolved outcomes. Practical implications and recommendations are discussed.

</details>


### [126] [DreamProver: Evolving Transferable Lemma Libraries via a Wake-Sleep Theorem-Proving Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26311)
*Youyuan Zhang,Jialiang Sun,Hangrui Bi,Chuqin Geng,Wenjie Ma,Zhaoyu Li,Xujie Si*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DreamProver提出了一种基于“觉醒-睡眠”程序归纳范式的框架，用于发现可重用的引理以进行形式化定理证明，通过迭代优化引理库，在多个数学基准上显著提高了证明成功率并降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖固定引理库或生成特定中间引理，缺乏适应性和通用性，DreamProver旨在发现高阶、可迁移的通用引理以提升定理证明的效率与泛化能力。

Method: 采用“觉醒-睡眠”两阶段循环：觉醒阶段使用当前引理库尝试证明定理并生成候选引理；睡眠阶段对候选引理进行抽象、精炼和整合，优化引理库。

Result: 在多个数学基准测试中，DreamProver显著提升了证明成功率，生成了更简洁的证明，并减少了计算开销。

Conclusion: DreamProver通过动态演化紧凑且可重用的引理库，有效增强了形式化定理证明的自动化与泛化能力。

Abstract: We introduce DreamProver, an agentic framework that leverages a "wake-sleep" program induction paradigm to discover reusable lemmas for formal theorem proving. Existing approaches either rely on fixed lemma libraries, which limit adaptability, or synthesize highly specific intermediate lemmas tailored to individual theorems, thereby lacking generality. DreamProver addresses this gap through an iterative two-stage process. In the wake stage, DreamProver attempts to prove theorems from a training set using the current lemma library while proposing new candidate lemmas. In the "sleep" stage, it abstracts, refines, and consolidates these candidates to compress and optimize the library. Through this alternating cycle, DreamProver progressively evolves a compact set of high-level, transferable lemmas that can be effectively used to prove unseen theorems in related domains. Experimental results demonstrate that DreamProver substantially improves proof success rates across a diverse set of mathematical benchmarks, while also producing more concise proofs and reducing computational cost.

</details>


### [127] [Benchmarking the Safety of Large Language Models for Robotic Health Attendant Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26577)
*Mahiro Nakao,Kazuhiro Takemoto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究评估了72个大语言模型（LLM）在机器人健康助手环境中的安全性，发现平均违规率高达54.4%，且开源模型安全性显著低于专有模型。医疗领域微调未能显著提升安全性，现有方法尚不足以支持临床安全部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于大语言模型越来越多地被考虑用于控制机器人健康助手，但其在此类敏感场景中的安全性尚未明确，因此需要系统评估其伦理合规性和潜在风险。

Method: 构建了一个包含270条基于美国医学会伦理原则的有害指令的数据集，涵盖九类禁止行为，并在机器人健康助手模拟环境中对72个大语言模型进行测试，分析不同模型在各类指令下的违规率及其影响因素。

Result: 所有模型的平均违规率为54.4%，超过一半的模型违规率超过50%；专有模型（中位数23.7%）明显比开源模型（72.8%）更安全；模型规模和发布时间是开源模型安全性的主要决定因素；医疗领域微调未带来显著安全增益；基于提示的防御策略仅轻微降低最不安全模型的违规率。

Conclusion: 必须将安全性评估作为大语言模型在机器人健康助手应用中开发与部署的首要标准，当前多数模型的安全性水平尚无法满足临床部署要求。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly considered for deployment as the control component of robotic health attendants, yet their safety in this context remains poorly characterized. We introduce a dataset of 270 harmful instructions spanning nine prohibited behavior categories grounded in the American Medical Association Principles of Medical Ethics, and use it to evaluate 72 LLMs in a simulation environment based on the Robotic Health Attendant framework. The mean violation rate across all models was 54.4\%, with more than half exceeding 50\%, and violation rates varied substantially across behavior categories, with superficially plausible instructions such as device manipulation and emergency delay proving harder to refuse than overtly destructive ones. Model size and release date were the primary determinants of safety performance among open-weight models, and proprietary models were substantially safer than open-weight counterparts (median 23.7\% versus 72.8\%). Medical domain fine-tuning conferred no significant overall safety benefit, and a prompt-based defense strategy produced only a modest reduction in violation rates among the least safe models, leaving absolute violation rates at levels that would preclude safe clinical deployment. These findings demonstrate that safety evaluation must be treated as a first-class criterion in the development and deployment of LLMs for robotic health attendants.

</details>


### [128] [Human-in-the-Loop Benchmarking of Heterogeneous LLMs for Automated Competency Assessment in Secondary Level Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26607)
*Jatin Bhusal,Nancy Mahatha,Aayush Acharya,Raunak Regmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种“人在回路中”的基准框架，评估多个大语言模型（LLM）在中学数学评估自动化中的有效性，发现架构兼容性比参数规模更重要，LLM目前尚不适合自主认证，但可在人工监督下提供辅助支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着能力本位教育（CBE）的推广，从分数评估转向定性能力映射给教师带来手动操作负担，亟需自动化解决方案。

Method: 基于尼泊尔十年级选修数学课程，构建涵盖四个主题和四项跨领域能力的多维评分标准，使用开源模型（Eagle、Orion）与闭源前沿模型（Nova、Lyra）组成的多提供方集成系统进行测试，并以两位资深数学教师的评分为基准（kappa_w = 0.8652）进行对比。

Result: Gemini系列的稀疏专家混合模型（Sparse MoE）达到“一般一致”（kappa_w ~ 0.38），而更大的Orion（70B）模型却表现为“无一致”（kappa_w = -0.0261），表明在指令约束任务中，架构对齐程度优于参数规模的影响。

Conclusion: 大语言模型虽尚未适合独立用于认证评估，但在“人在回路”框架下可有效支持初步证据提取，为教育评估提供高价值辅助工具。

Abstract: As Competency-Based Education (CBE) is gaining traction around the world, the shift from marks-based assessment to qualitative competency mapping is a manual challenge for educators. This paper tackles the bottleneck issue by suggesting a "Human-in-the-Loop" benchmarking framework to assess the effectiveness of multiple LLMs in automating secondary-level mathematics assessment. Based on the Grade 10 Optional Mathematics curriculum in Nepal, we created a multi-dimensional rubric for four topics and four cross-cutting competencies: Comprehension, Knowledge, Operational Fluency, and Behavior and Correlation.
  The multi-provider ensemble, consisted of open-weight models -- Eagle (Llama 3.1-8B) and Orion (Llama 3.3-70B) -- and proprietary frontier models Nova (Gemini 2.5 Flash) and Lyra (Gemini 3 Pro), was benchmarked against a ground truth defined by two senior mathematics faculty members (kappa_w = 0.8652). The findings show a marked "Architecture-compatibility gap". Although the Gemini-based Mixture-of-Experts (Sparse MoE) models achieved "Fair Agreement" (kappa_w ~ 0.38), the larger Orion (70B) model exhibited "No Agreement" (kappa_w = -0.0261), suggesting that architectural compliance with instruction constraints outweighs the scale of raw parameters in rubric-constrained tasks. We conclude that while LLMs are not yet suitable for autonomous certification, they provide high-value assistive support for preliminary evidence extraction within a "Human-in-the-Loop" framework.

</details>


### [129] [When to Vote, When to Rewrite: Disagreement-Guided Strategy Routing for Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26644)
*Zhimin Lin,Yixin Ji,Jinpeng Li,Yu Luo,Dong Li,Junhua Fang,Juntao Li,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的测试时扩展框架，通过实例级路由选择不同策略，基于输出分歧动态分配计算资源，在数学推理任务上提升了准确性并降低了采样成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型在数学任务上表现强但对难题仍不可靠，现有测试时扩展方法计算成本高且在难题上收益递减，需更有效的实例级策略选择机制。

Method: 将测试时扩展建模为实例级路由问题，根据输出分歧程度选择轻量求解、多数投票或基于重写的重构策略，实现动态策略分配。

Result: 在七个数学基准和三个模型上实验表明，相比现有方法，在提高3%-7%准确性的同时减少了采样成本。

Conclusion: 该框架有效利用输出分歧信号进行策略路由，实现了更高的效率与性能平衡，为测试时扩展提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) achieve strong performance on mathematical reasoning tasks but remain unreliable on challenging instances. Existing test-time scaling methods, such as repeated sampling, self-correction, and tree search, improve performance at the cost of increased computation, yet often exhibit diminishing returns on hard problems. We observe that output disagreement is strongly correlated with instance difficulty and prediction correctness, providing a useful signal for guiding instance-level strategy selection at test time. Based on this insight, we propose a training-free framework that formulates test-time scaling as an instance-level routing problem, rather than allocating more computation within a single strategy, dynamically selecting among different scaling strategies based on output disagreement. The framework applies lightweight resolution for consistent cases, majority voting for moderate disagreement, and rewriting-based reformulation for highly ambiguous instances. Experiments on seven mathematical benchmarks and three models show that our method improves accuracy by 3% - 7% while reducing sampling cost compared to existing approaches.

</details>


### [130] [SciHorizon-DataEVA: An Agentic System for AI-Readiness Evaluation of Heterogeneous Scientific Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26645)
*Dianyu Liu,Chuan Qin,Xi Chen,Xiaohan Li,Wenxi Xu,Yuyang Wang,Xin Chen,Yuanchun Zhou,Hengshu Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SciHorizon-DataEVA的新型智能体系统，用于可扩展地评估异构科学数据的AI就绪性，并引入了Sci-TQA2原则和多智能体评估方法，在多个科学领域中验证了其有效性与通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习模型在科学发现中的应用受限于科学数据的AI就绪性，但目前缺乏可扩展且系统的评估机制。

Method: 提出了Sci-TQA2原则，将AI就绪性分为四个维度，并开发了基于分层多智能体的Sci-TQA2-Eval评估方法，通过动态构建数据集感知的评估规范进行可扩展评估。

Result: 在跨多个领域的科学数据集上进行了大量实验，验证了该方法在原则性AI就绪性评估中的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: SciHorizon-DataEVA为科学数据的AI就绪性提供了系统、可扩展的评估框架，有助于推动AI-for-Science的发展。

Abstract: AI-for-Science (AI4Science) is increasingly transforming scientific discovery by embedding machine learning models into prediction, simulation, and hypothesis generation workflows across domains. However, the effectiveness of these models is fundamentally constrained by the AI-readiness of scientific data, for which no scalable and systematic evaluation mechanism currently exists. In this work, we propose SciHorizon-DataEVA, a novel agentic system to scalable AI-readiness evaluation of heterogeneous scientific data. At the evaluation-criteria level, we introduce the Sci-TQA2 principles, which organize AI-readiness into four complementary dimensions: Governance Trustworthiness, Data Quality, AI Compatibility, and Scientific Adaptability. Each dimension is decomposed into measurable atomic elements that enable fine-grained and executable assessment. To operationalize these principles at scale, we develop Sci-TQA2-Eval, a hierarchical multi-agent evaluation approach orchestrated through a directed, cyclic workflow. Our Sci-TQA2-Eval dynamically constructs dataset-aware evaluation specifications by combining lightweight dataset profiling, applicability-aware metric activation, and knowledge-augmented planning grounded in domain constraints and dataset-paper signals. These specifications are executed through an adaptive, tool-centric evaluation mechanism with built-in verification and self-correction, enabling scalable and reliable assessment across heterogeneous scientific data. Extensive experiments on scientific datasets spanning multiple domains demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of SciHorizon-DataEVA for principled AI-readiness evaluation.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [FalconApp: Rapid iPhone Deployment of End-to-End Perception via Automatically Labeled Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25949)
*Yan Miao,Will Shen,Sayan Mitra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FalconApp是一个iPhone应用，通过端到端的前后端流程，将刚性物体的短时间手持视频转化为用于掩码检测和6自由度位姿估计的感知模块，利用快速移动部署和逼真的自动标注生成高质量感知模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少对大规模手动标注数据的依赖，加速机器人感知模型的开发与部署。

Method: 提出FalconApp，包含移动端视频采集、GSplat资产重建、合成多样化背景下的逼真图像，并自动生成带掩码和位姿真值的训练数据，训练并部署轻量感知模型至iPhone。

Result: 在五个不同几何与外观的刚性物体上验证，平均每个物体仅需约20分钟合成数据生成与训练，iPhone上端到端延迟约30ms，在4/5个物体上位姿精度优于PnP基线方法。

Conclusion: FalconApp实现了从移动设备采集到高性能感知模型部署的快速闭环，为机器人感知提供了高效、实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable perception for robotics depends on large-scale labeled data, yet real-world datasets rely on heavy manual annotation and are time-consuming to produce. We present FalconApp, an iPhone app with an end-to-end frontend-backend pipeline that turns a short handheld capture of a rigid object into a perception module for mask detection and 6-DoF pose estimation. Our core contribution is a rapid mobile deployment pipeline paired with a photorealistic auto-labeling workflow: from a user-captured video of an object, FalconApp reconstructs an editable GSplat asset, composites it with diverse photorealistic backgrounds, renders synthetic images with ground-truth masks and poses, trains the perception module, and deploys it back to the iPhone frontend. Experiments across five rigid objects with diverse geometry and appearance show that FalconApp produces usable perception models with about 20 minutes of synthetic-data generation and training per object on average, around 30 ms end-to-end on-device latency on iPhone, and better overall pose accuracy than a PnP baseline on 4 / 5 objects in both simulation and real-world evaluation.

</details>


### [132] [A Scaled Three-Vehicle Platooning Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25963)
*Kaiyue Lu,Qiaoxuan Zhang,Yukun Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种用于自动驾驶编队研究的缩比多车实验平台，重点解决车辆编队中领航-跟随协调控制问题，特别是在变道等动态操作中的路径跟踪稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆编队在提升交通效率、节能和道路安全方面具有潜力，但在动态 maneuvers 中存在路径跟踪不稳定、偏差传播等问题，需要鲁棒的纵横向控制。现有全尺寸实地测试成本高、风险大，纯仿真缺乏物理真实性，因此需要一个兼具安全性、低成本与物理真实性的实验平台。

Method: 开发了一个包含一辆人工操控领航车和两辆自主跟随车的缩比多车平台，用于开展可控且可重复的领航-跟随协调实验。该平台支持控制器快速原型验证、多智能体自主性研究，并结合了比仿真更强的物理真实性和比实车测试更低的成本与更高安全性。

Result: 该平台为车辆编队中的协同控制和人机共驾研究提供了有效的实验手段，能够在动态工况下评估控制算法对路径跟踪和车队稳定性的表现。

Conclusion: 所提出的缩比多车平台是一种高效、安全且经济的研究工具，适用于自动驾驶编队中协同控制策略的开发与验证，尤其适合在复杂动态场景中进行人机交互与控制器性能测试。

Abstract: Vehicle platooning has attracted increasing attention as a promising approach to improve traffic efficiency, energy consumption, and roadway safety through coordinated multi-vehicle operation. A key challenge in platooning lies in maintaining stable and accurate path tracking during dynamic maneuvers such as lane changes, where lateral deviations and heading disturbances generated by the lead vehicle may propagate downstream to following vehicles. Robust longitudinal and lateral control systems are therefore essential not only for individual vehicle tracking performance, but also for overall platoon stability. For experimental studies, the Intelligent Mobility and Robotics Lab (IMRL) develops a scaled multi-vehicle platform for autonomous platooning research, with a particular emphasis on cooperative control and human-in-the-loop autonomy. This platform consists of one human-operable lead vehicle and two autonomous followers, enabling controlled and repeatable experiments on leader-follower coordination. Compared with full-scale field testing, this scaled platform offers a safer, lower-cost, and more flexible environment for rapid prototyping, controller validation, and multi-agent autonomy studies, while providing stronger physical realism than purely simulation-based evaluations.

</details>


### [133] [Multi-Periodogram Velocity Estimation with Irregular Reference Signals for Robot-Aided ISAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25974)
*Yi Geng,Pan Cao,Ting Zeng,Yongqian Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多周期图的机器人辅助感知与通信中的速度估计方法，利用不规则5G/6G参考信号实现标准兼容的速度估计，显著提升低信噪比下的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人辅助的ISAC系统中，移动机器人只能机会性地重用不规则的5G/6G参考信号进行感知，传统速度估计方法在低信噪比和不规则时域模式下性能受限，亟需一种无需新增专用感知信号的标准兼容算法。

Method: 将由不规则时域模式引起的速度谱分解为周期性峰值分量和幅度整形（加权）分量，基于此结构设计多周期图速度估计算法，完全复用现有通信标准中的参考信号，无需3GPP标准修改或新增专用感知资源。

Result: 相比传统周期图处理方法，在10%漏检率下实现了3 dB的信噪比增益，低信噪比鲁棒性显著增强，同时虚假报警减少了51%。

Conclusion: 所提方法在不改变现有通信标准的前提下，有效提升了机器人辅助ISAC系统中基于不规则参考信号的速度估计性能，具备良好的实用性和标准化前景。

Abstract: This paper addresses velocity estimation within robot-aided integrated sensing and communications (ISAC), where mobile robots act as sensing nodes but can only opportunistically reuse irregular 5G/6G reference signals (RSs). We show that the velocity profile induced by such irregular time-domain patterns can be decomposed into a periodic-peak component and an amplitude-shaping (weighting) component. Leveraging this structure, we propose a multi-periodogram velocity estimation algorithm that is standard-compliant and does not require new sensing-dedicated RSs or 3GPP modifications. Simulation results demonstrate that, compared with conventional periodogram processing, the proposed method improves low-SNR robustness by achieving a 3 dB SNR gain at the 10% missed-detection rate and reducing false alarms by 51%.

</details>


### [134] [FlowS: One-Step Motion Prediction via Local Transport Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26065)
*Leandro Di Bella,Adrian Munteanu,Bruno Cornelis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为FlowS的条件流匹配框架，通过“局部传输条件”实现单步生成式运动预测，在保证高精度和多模态未来的同时满足严格延迟限制，在Waymo数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽能实现高精度和多模态运动预测，但需要大量去噪步骤，难以满足实时性要求；现有方法在单步生成中存在离散化误差大、训练信号方差高等问题。

Method: 提出局部传输条件策略：1）基于场景的在线学习先验生成K个靠近合理未来的锚定轨迹，将模式发现转化为局部修正；2）构建步长一致的位移场，通过半群自洽性保证单步推理继承多步精度，并以直线路径锚定降低训练方差。

Result: 在Waymo Open Motion Dataset上，FlowS以单步推理实现了Soft mAP 0.4804和mAP 0.4703的SOTA性能，支持75FPS的实时推理。

Conclusion: 局部传输条件使单步生成式运动预测在安全关键型自主系统中变得实用，解决了精度、多样性与低延迟之间的矛盾。

Abstract: Generative motion prediction must satisfy three simultaneous requirements for real-world autonomy: high accuracy, diverse multimodal futures, and strictly bounded latency. Diffusion models meet the first two but violate the third, requiring tens to hundreds of denoising steps. We identify a conditioning strategy that resolves this tension: \textit{single-step integration is accurate when the underlying transport problem is local}. A model that must both discover the correct behavioral mode and traverse a long displacement in one step accumulates large discretization errors; conditioning the base distribution to lie near plausible futures reduces the problem to short-range refinement, the regime where a single Euler step suffices. We instantiate this \emph{local transport conditioning} in FlowS, a conditional flow matching framework with two mechanisms. First, an online, scene-conditioned learned prior emits $K$ calibrated anchor trajectories per agent, each already near a plausible future, converting mode discovery into local correction. Second, a step-consistent displacement field enforces semigroup self-consistency, guaranteeing that a single step inherits multi-step accuracy. Crucially, anchoring this field at learned priors along straight-line paths yields a {stable, low-variance} training target, unlike prior self-consistency methods that suffer from {high-variance bootstrap} signals on curved diffusion paths. On the Waymo Open Motion Dataset, FlowS achieves state-of-the-art Soft mAP {(0.4804) and mAP (0.4703) with ensemble at 75\,FPS} with single-step inference, demonstrating that local transport conditioning makes one-step generative motion prediction practical for safety-critical autonomy. Code and pretrained models will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [135] [Lights Out: A Nighttime UAV Localization Framework Using Thermal Imagery and Semantic 3D Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26201)
*Ryan Allen,Melissa Greeff*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于语义重投影的夜间无人机定位框架，通过将热成像与白天构建的语义3D地图对齐，实现了在无GNSS条件下的可靠定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无人机在无GNSS且夜间环境下因白天RGB图像与夜间热成像之间模态差异大而导致的定位难题。

Method: 在共享语义域中进行定位，利用双向对称重投影目标函数，并引入混淆感知加权以提升分割不确定性下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在6.5公里的真实夜间飞行轨迹上测试，相对于RTK GNSS真值，系统实现2.18米的去偏RMSE2D和1.52米的中位RMSE2D。性能与语义边缘信息高度相关，大误差集中在语义模糊区域。

Conclusion: 语义重投影为仅使用热成像实现全球参考的夜间无人机定位提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable backup localization for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) operating in GNSS-denied nighttime conditions remains an open challenge due to the severe modality gap between daytime RGB maps and nighttime thermal imagery. This work presents a semantic reprojection framework for map-relative nighttime UAV localization by aligning segmented thermal observations with a globally referenced, semantically labeled 3D map constructed from daytime RGB data. Rather than relying on appearance-based correspondence, localization is formulated in a shared semantic domain and solved via a symmetric bidirectional reprojection objective with confusion-aware weighting to improve robustness under segmentation uncertainty. The approach is evaluated offline across 6.5 km of nighttime, real-world UAV flight trajectories in urban and semi-structured environments. Relative to RTK GNSS ground truth, the system achieves a bias-corrected RMSE2D of 2.18 m and a median RMSE2D of 1.52 m. Results show that localization performance is strongly correlated with the availability of semantic edge evidence and that large-error events are spatially localized to semantically ambiguous areas rather than uniformly distributed. These findings indicate that semantic reprojection offers a promising pathway toward globally referenced nighttime UAV localization using thermal imagery alone.

</details>


### [136] [2D and 3D Grasp Planners for the GET Asymmetrical Gripper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26212)
*Andrew Goldberg,Ethan Ransing,Anton Kourakin,Cael Magner,Edward H. Adelson,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种抓取规划方法GET-2D-1.0和GET-3D-1.0，分别基于单视角RGB-D图像和三维网格模型，实验表明GET-2D-1.0在效率和性能上显著优于基线方法，而GET-3D-1.0虽性能略优但计算成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升不对称夹爪在复杂场景下的抓取成功率，并在计算效率与抓取性能之间取得更好平衡。

Method: GET-2D-1.0利用单视角RGB-D图像，结合Ferrari-Canny度量和新的采样策略；GET-3D-1.0则采用基于网格的3D夹爪模型和光线追踪技术进行抓取规划。

Result: GET-2D-1.0相比边界框基线在提升抓取成功率、抗晃动和抗力能力方面提高超过40%；GET-3D-1.0在前两项指标上略有提升，但平均规划耗时17秒，远高于GET-2D-1.0的683毫秒。

Conclusion: GET-2D-1.0在保持极低计算时间的同时显著提升抓取性能，适合实时应用；GET-3D-1.0虽精度略高但耗时较长，适用于对精度要求更高的场景。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce GET-2D-1.0, a fast grasp planner for the GET asymmetrical gripper that operates from a single-view RGB-D image, using the Ferrari-Canny metric and a novel sampling strategy, and GET-3D-1.0, a mesh-based method using a 3D gripper model and ray-tracing. We evaluate both grasp planners against baselines with physical experiments, which suggest that GET-2D-1.0 can improve over a bounding box baseline by over 40% in lift success, shake survival, and force resistance. Experiments with GET-3D-1.0 suggest slight improvement compared to GET-2D-1.0 on lift success and shake survival, but are more computationally expensive, averaging 17 seconds of planning compared to 683 ms for GET-2D-1.0.

</details>


### [137] [Split over $n$ resource sharing problem: Are fewer capable agents better than many simpler ones?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26374)
*Karthik Soma,Mohamed S. Talamali,Genki Miyauchi,Giovanni Beltrame,Heiko Hamann,Roderich Gross*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究了在多智能体系统中资源应集中于少数强大个体还是分配给多个简单个体的问题，通过多智能体覆盖案例分析发现资源分配方式对性能有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在资源受限的情况下，如何最优地分配资源以优化多智能体系统的性能。

Method: 提出n个资源共享问题模型，并在多智能体覆盖场景中进行形式化分析和计算机仿真。

Result: 形式化分析表明初始覆盖率随智能体数量增加而增长；但当智能体速度与其半径成比例下降时，不同规模组表现相当，若速度与足迹成比例下降，则单个智能体最优；仿真显示资源分割会增加个体失败率。

Conclusion: 资源分配策略需根据具体约束条件权衡，模型有助于确定最优的分布程度并指导资源受限下的多智能体系统设计。

Abstract: In multi-agent systems, should limited resources be concentrated into a few capable agents or distributed among many simpler ones? This work formulates the split over $n$ resource sharing problem where a group of $n$ agents equally shares a common resource (e.g., monetary budget, computational resources, physical size). We present a case study in multi-agent coverage where the area of the disk-shaped footprint of agents scales as $1/n$. A formal analysis reveals that the initial coverage rate grows with $n$. However, if the speed of agents decreases proportionally with their radii, groups of all sizes perform equally well, whereas if it decreases proportionally with their footprints, a single agent performs best. We also present computer simulations in which resource splitting increases the failure rates of individual agents. The models and findings help identify optimal distributiveness levels and inform the design of multi-agent systems under resource constraints.

</details>


### [138] [Reactive Motion Generation via Phase-varying Neural Potential Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26450)
*Ahmet Tekden,Dimitrios Kanoulas,Aude Billard,Yasemin Bekiroglu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Phase-varying Neural Potential Functions (PNPF)的Learning-from-Demonstration框架，通过基于状态进展估计的相位变量来调节势函数，有效处理具有交叉点的轨迹，并在点到点、周期性和6D运动任务中表现出优越的鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的一阶动力系统无法处理轨迹交叉的任务，而二阶系统或基于相位的方法在抗干扰或闭环恢复方面存在局限，因此需要一种既能处理状态重复访问又能保持稳定控制的新方法。

Method: 引入PNPF框架，将势函数依赖于从状态进展中直接估计的相位变量，而非开环时间变量，从而在保持局部向量场稳定性的同时解决轨迹交叉问题。

Result: PNPF在具有交叉点的轨迹上优于现有基线方法，能够泛化到多种任务类型（点到点、周期性、6D运动），并在真实机器人操作中展现出对外部扰动的强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PNPF通过状态驱动的相位变量实现了对复杂轨迹的有效学习与控制，克服了传统方法在交叉路径和扰动恢复方面的不足，适用于实际机器人应用。

Abstract: Dynamical systems (DS) methods for Learning-from-Demonstration (LfD) provide stable, continuous policies from few demonstrations. First-order dynamical systems (DS) are effective for many point-to-point and periodic tasks, as long as a unique velocity is defined for each state. For tasks with intersections (e.g., drawing an "8"), extensions such as second-order dynamics or phase variables are often used. However, by incorporating velocity, second-order models become sensitive to disturbances near intersections, as velocity is used to disambiguate motion direction. Moreover, this disambiguation may fail when nearly identical position-velocity pairs correspond to different onward motions. In contrast, phase-based methods rely on open-loop time or phase variables, which limit their ability to recover after perturbations. We introduce Phase-varying Neural Potential Functions (PNPF), an LfD framework that conditions a potential function on a phase variable which is estimated directly from state progression, rather than on open-loop temporal inputs. This phase variable allows the system to handle state revisits, while the learned potential function generates local vector fields for reactive and stable control. PNPF generalizes effectively across point-to-point, periodic, and full 6D motion tasks, outperforms existing baselines on trajectories with intersections, and demonstrates robust performance in real-time robotic manipulation under external disturbances.

</details>


### [139] [Alter-Art: Exploring Embodied Artistic Creation through a Robot Avatar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26473)
*Do Won Park,Samuele Bordini,Giorgio Grioli,Manuel G. Catalano,Antonio Bicchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了“Alter-Art”艺术创作新范式，通过机器人化身Alter-Ego实现艺术家以第一人称视角远程操控机器人进行舞蹈、戏剧和绘画创作，探索具身化对创造力、身份认同和环境互动的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前艺术机器人多被用作自主创作者或合作者，导致艺术家与机器之间存在鸿沟。本文旨在弥合这一差距，使艺术家能突破身体限制，通过机器人实现具身化的艺术表达。

Method: 开发了名为Alter-Ego的机器人化身系统，结合沉浸式遥操作与顺应性驱动技术，支持艺术家以第一人称视角操控机器人进行实际物理世界的艺术创作，并通过定性艺术反馈分析具身化体验。

Result: 研究表明艺术家能迅速在机器人身体中建立存在感；机器人的物理约束会影响创作过程，并在不同艺术领域中表现出差异；具身化被确认为影响创造性主体性和交互的关键因素。

Conclusion: 具身化是连接人类艺术家与机器人的重要设计原则，不仅拓展了远程临场感与可及性艺术表达的可能性，也为社会机器人学提供了新的视角。

Abstract: As with every emerging technology, new tools in the hands of artists reshape the nature of artwork creation. Current frameworks for robotics in arts deploy the robot as an autonomous creator or a collaborator, thus leaving a certain gap between the human artist and the machine. Now, we stand at the dawn of an era where artists can escape physical limitations and reshape their creative identity by inhabiting an alternative body. This new paradigm allows artists not only to command a robot remotely, but also to {\it be} a robot, to see and feel through it, experiencing a new embodied reality. Unlike virtual reality, where art is created in a digital dimension, in this case art creation is still firmly grounded in the material world: clay molded by mechanical hands, paint swept across a canvas or gestures performed on a physical stage alongside human actors. Through the robot avatar Alter-Ego, we explore the Alter-Art paradigm in dance, theater, and painting; it integrates immersive teleoperation and compliant actuation to enable a first-person creative experience. Analyzing qualitative artistic feedback, we investigate how embodiment shapes creative agency, identity and interaction with the environment. Our findings suggest that artists rapidly develop a sense of presence within the robotic body. The robot's physical constraints influence the creative process, manifesting differently across artistic domains. We highlight embodiment as a central design principle, contributing to social robotics and expanding the possibilities for telepresence and accessible artistic expression.

</details>


### [140] [HiPAN: Hierarchical Posture-Adaptive Navigation for Quadruped Robots in Unstructured 3D Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26504)
*Jeil Jeong,Minsung Yoon,Seokryun Choi,Heechan Shin,Taegeun Yang,Sung-eui Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HiPAN的分层姿态自适应导航框架，直接基于机载深度图像实现四足机器人在非结构化3D环境中的高效导航，结合路径引导课程学习策略，显著提升了长距离导航的成功率与路径效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统映射-规划流水线方法存在感知误差累积和计算开销高的问题，难以在资源受限平台上实现在复杂3D环境中的有效导航。

Method: 提出HiPAN框架，采用分层设计：高层策略生成平面速度和身体姿态指令，底层控制器实现姿态自适应运动控制；引入路径引导课程学习，逐步扩展导航视野范围。

Result: 在仿真中，HiPAN相比经典反应式规划器和端到端基线方法实现了更高的导航成功率和路径效率；真实世界实验验证了其在多样化非结构化3D环境中的适用性。

Conclusion: HiPAN能够有效实现目标导向、姿态自适应且鲁棒的长距离导航，适用于资源受限的四足机器人平台。

Abstract: Navigating quadruped robots in unstructured 3D environments poses significant challenges, requiring goal-directed motion, effective exploration to escape from local minima, and posture adaptation to traverse narrow, height-constrained spaces. Conventional approaches employ a sequential mapping-planning pipeline but suffer from accumulated perception errors and high computational overhead, restricting their applicability on resource-constrained platforms. To address these challenges, we propose Hierarchical Posture-Adaptive Navigation (HiPAN), a framework that operates directly on onboard depth images at deployment. HiPAN adopts a hierarchical design: a high-level policy generates strategic navigation commands (planar velocity and body posture), which are executed by a low-level, posture-adaptive locomotion controller. To mitigate myopic behaviors and facilitate long-horizon navigation, we introduce Path-Guided Curriculum Learning, which progressively extends the navigation horizon from reactive obstacle avoidance to strategic navigation. In simulation, HiPAN achieves higher navigation success rates and greater path efficiency than classical reactive planners and end-to-end baselines, while real-world experiments further validate its applicability across diverse, unstructured 3D environments.

</details>


### [141] [LLM-Flax : Generalizable Robotic Task Planning via Neuro-Symbolic Approaches with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26569)
*Seongmin Kim,Daegyu Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LLM-Flax是一个三阶段框架，利用本地部署的大型语言模型（LLM），仅需PDDL域文件即可自动完成神经符号任务规划中的规则生成、失败恢复和对象评分，完全消除手动干预，在MazeNamo基准上显著优于传统手动方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经符号任务规划在新领域部署时需要大量人工工作，包括手工编写规则和准备大量训练问题来训练GNN模型，成本高且效率低。本文旨在完全消除这些人工依赖，实现自动化部署。

Method: 提出LLM-Flax框架：第一阶段通过结构化提示与自校正机制自动生成松弛与互补规则；第二阶段引入基于可行性的预算策略进行LLM引导的失败恢复，控制API延迟开销；第三阶段用零样本LLM替代训练过的GNN进行对象重要性评分，无需训练数据。

Result: 在MazeNamo的8个基准测试中，LLM-Flax平均成功率（SR）达0.945，显著高于手动方法的0.828；在多个难度场景下表现更优，如12x12 Expert上从0.000提升至0.733，15x15 Hard上达到1.000；第三阶段验证了无需训练数据的可行性，但在大规模场景下面临上下文窗口限制。

Conclusion: LLM-Flax能有效消除神经符号规划中的三大人工负担，仅凭PDDL文件即可实现高性能自动部署，展示了LLM在任务规划自动化中的巨大潜力，但大规模应用仍受限于LLM的上下文长度。

Abstract: Deploying a neuro-symbolic task planner on a new domain today requires significant manual effort: a domain expert must author relaxation and complementary rules, and hundreds of training problems must be solved to supervise a Graph Neural Network (GNN) object scorer. We propose LLM-Flax, a three-stage framework that eliminates all three sources of manual effort using a locally hosted LLM given only a PDDL domain file. Stage 1 automatically generates relaxation and complementary rules via structured prompting with format validation and self-correction. Stage 2 introduces LLM-guided failure recovery with a feasibility-gated budget policy that explicitly reserves API latency cost before each LLM call, preventing the downstream relaxation fallback from being starved. Stage 3 replaces the domain-trained GNN entirely with zero-shot LLM object importance scoring, requiring no training data. We evaluate all three stages on the MazeNamo benchmark across 10x10, 12x12, and 15x15 grids (8 benchmarks total). LLM-Flax achieves average SR 0.945 versus the manual baseline's 0.828 (+0.117), matching or outperforming manual rules on every one of the eight benchmarks. On 12x12 Expert, LLM-Flax attains SR 0.733 where the manual planner fails entirely (SR 0.000); on 15x15 Hard, it achieves SR 1.000 versus Manual's 0.900. Stage 3 demonstrates feasibility (SR 0.720 on 12x12 Hard with no training data) but faces a context-window bottleneck at scale, pointing to the primary open challenge for future work.

</details>


### [142] [STAR-Filter: Efficient Convex Free-Space Approximation via Starshaped Set Filtering in Noisy Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26626)
*Yuwei Wu,Yichen Zhao,Dexter Ong,Vijay Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为STAR-Filter的轻量级框架，利用星形集构造快速生成无碰撞空间中的凸区域，相比传统方法在计算效率和对噪声的鲁棒性方面表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于迭代优化的凸多面体膨胀方法在复杂或噪声环境中效率低、对初始化敏感且依赖精确几何模型，难以满足实时机器人规划需求。

Method: 提出STAR-Filter框架，通过构建星形集作为快速滤波器，利用障碍物点作为主动支撑约束来生成可行的凸区域，减少冗余计算并提升对传感器噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: 理论与数值分析验证了该方法在不同复杂度环境下的有效性；仿真结果表明其在真实世界噪声和大规模数据下具有最低计算时间，并降低了多面体生成的保守性；成功应用于安全飞行走廊生成与敏捷四旋翼规划。

Conclusion: STAR-Filter是一种高效、鲁棒且轻量的凸区域生成方法，适用于复杂和噪声环境下的机器人运动规划。

Abstract: Approximating collision-free space is fundamental to robot planning in complex environments. Convex geometric representations, such as polytopes and ellipsoids, are widely employed due to their structural properties, which can be easily integrated with convex optimization. Iterative optimization-based inflation methods can generate large volume polytopes in cluttered environments, but their efficiency degrades as the obstacle set becomes more complex or when sensor data are noisy. These methods are also sensitive to initialization and often rely on accurate geometric models. In this paper, we propose the STAR-Filter, a lightweight framework that employs starshaped set construction as a fast filter for convex region generation in collision-free space. By identifying obstacle points as active supporting constraints, the proposed method significantly reduces redundant computation while preserving feasibility and robustness to sensor noise. We provide theoretical and numerical analyses that characterize the structural properties of the starshaped set and proposed pipeline in environments of varying complexity. Simulation results show that the proposed framework achieves the lowest computation time and reduces conservativeness in polytope generation for real-world noisy and large-scale data. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the framework for Safe Flight Corridor (SFC) generation and agile quadrotor planning in noisy environments.

</details>


### [143] [ATLAS: An Annotation Tool for Long-horizon Robotic Action Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26637)
*Sergej Stanovcic,Daniel Sliwowski,Dongheui Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为ATLAS的机器人动作分割标注工具，支持多模态数据同步可视化和多种机器人数据格式，显著提高了标注效率和时序精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的标注工具在处理长周期机器人演示数据时存在局限，缺乏对机器人特有时间序列信号的原生支持，且难以适配不同数据格式，限制了动作分割和策略学习的研究。

Method: 设计并实现了一个名为ATLAS的标注工具，具备多模态数据（如多视角视频和本体感知信号）的时间同步可视化功能，原生支持ROS bags和RLDS等机器人数据格式，并通过模块化数据抽象层支持扩展；采用以键盘为中心的界面设计以提升标注效率。

Result: 在接触密集型装配任务实验中，ATLAS相比ELAN平均每个动作标注时间减少至少6%，加入时间序列数据后比仅使用视觉的工具时序对齐精度提高超过2.8%，边界误差降低五倍。

Conclusion: ATLAS是一种高效、灵活且专为长周期机器人动作分割设计的标注工具，能够显著提升标注速度和准确性，推动机器人学习相关研究的发展。

Abstract: Annotating long-horizon robotic demonstrations with precise temporal action boundaries is crucial for training and evaluating action segmentation and manipulation policy learning methods. Existing annotation tools, however, are often limited: they are designed primarily for vision-only data, do not natively support synchronized visualization of robot-specific time-series signals (e.g., gripper state or force/torque), or require substantial effort to adapt to different dataset formats. In this paper, we introduce ATLAS, an annotation tool tailored for long-horizon robotic action segmentation. ATLAS provides time-synchronized visualization of multi-modal robotic data, including multi-view video and proprioceptive signals, and supports annotation of action boundaries, action labels, and task outcomes. The tool natively handles widely used robotics dataset formats such as ROS bags and the Reinforcement Learning Dataset (RLDS) format, and provides direct support for specific datasets such as REASSEMBLE. ATLAS can be easily extended to new formats via a modular dataset abstraction layer. Its keyboard-centric interface minimizes annotation effort and improves efficiency. In experiments on a contact-rich assembly task, ATLAS reduced the average per-action annotation time by at least 6% compared to ELAN, while the inclusion of time-series data improved temporal alignment with expert annotations by more than 2.8% and decreased boundary error fivefold compared to vision-only annotation tools.

</details>


### [144] [Atomic-Probe Governance for Skill Updates in Compositional Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26689)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,John See,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的交叉版本交换协议，用于研究机器人技能库更新时组合性能的变化，发现存在“主导技能效应”，并提出基于原子质量探测的混合选择器，在零或低额外成本下有效预测技能更新的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能组合方法假设技能库在测试时是静态不变的，但实际部署中技能会不断更新。本文旨在研究当某个技能被替换时，组合行为如何变化，并建立适用于动态技能库的治理机制。

Method: 引入配对采样交叉版本交换协议，在robosuite操作任务上评估技能更新对组合结果的影响；提出原子质量探测和混合选择器（结合零成本的单技能探测与部分组合重验证）来预测更新效果，并分析其在144个技能更新决策中的Pareto前沿。

Result: 在双臂插销任务中发现显著的主导技能效应（成功率达86.7% vs 其他≤26.7%），该技能是否参与组合可带来高达+50个百分点的成功率变化；行为距离指标无法识别主导技能；原子质量探测在T6任务上以零成本达到全重验证64.6%的效果（后者为87.5%），混合选择器（m=10）以46%成本缩小差距至约12个百分点；跨任务平均下，原子探测在混合oracle条件下与全重验证相差仅3pp。

Conclusion: 原子质量探测是首个有原则且可部署的技能更新治理原语，能高效预测技能更新对组合策略的影响，为动态演化的机器人技能库提供了实用的监控与选择机制。

Abstract: Skill libraries in deployed robotic systems are continually updated through fine-tuning, fresh demonstrations, or domain adaptation, yet existing typed-composition methods (BLADE, SymSkill, Generative Skill Chaining) treat the library as frozen at test time and do not analyze how composition outcomes change when a skill is replaced. We introduce a paired-sampling cross-version swap protocol on robosuite manipulation tasks to characterize this dimension of compositional skill learning. On a dual-arm peg-in-hole task we discover a dominant-skill effect: one ECM achieves 86.7% atomic success rate while every other ECM is at or below 26.7%, and whether this dominant ECM enters a composition shifts the success rate by up to +50pp. We characterize the boundary on a simpler pick task where all atomic policies saturate at 100% and the effect is undefined. Across three tasks we further find that off-policy behavioral distance metrics fail to identify the dominant ECM, ruling out the natural cheap predictor. We propose an atomic-quality probe and a Hybrid Selector combining per-skill probes (zero per-decision cost) with selective composition revalidation (full cost), and characterize its Pareto frontier on 144 skill-update decisions. On T6 the atomic-only probe sits 23pp below full revalidation (64.6% vs 87.5% oracle match) at zero per-decision cost; a Hybrid Selector with m=10 closes most of that gap to ~12pp at 46% of full-revalidation cost. On the cross-task average over 144 events, atomic-only is within 3pp of full revalidation under a mixed-oracle caveat. The atomic-quality probe is, to our knowledge, the first principled, deployment-ready primitive for skill-update governance in compositional robot policies.

</details>


### [145] [Unified 4D World Action Modeling from Video Priors with Asynchronous Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26694)
*Jun Guo,Qiwei Li,Peiyan Li,Zilong Chen,Nan Sun,Yifei Su,Heyun Wang,Yuan Zhang,Xinghang Li,Huaping Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: X-WAM是一种统一的4D世界模型，结合了实时机器人动作执行与高保真4D世界生成（视频+3D重建），通过轻量级结构适配和异步噪声采样（ANS）在动作效率与建模质量之间实现平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一世界模型（如UWM）仅建模2D像素空间，难以兼顾动作执行效率与高质量世界建模，限制了其在复杂机器人任务中的应用。

Method: 利用预训练视频扩散模型的视觉先验，预测多视角RGB-D视频；通过复制Diffusion Transformer的最后几层构建专用深度预测分支，实现高效的4D结构重建；提出异步噪声采样（ANS），在推理时采用不同步的去噪调度，快速解码动作并完整生成高质量视频，并在训练时从联合分布中采样以对齐推理分布。

Result: 在超过5800小时机器人数据上预训练后，X-WAM在RoboCasa和RoboTwin 2.0基准上分别达到79.2%和90.7%的平均成功率，同时在视觉与几何指标上超越现有方法，实现高质量4D重建与生成。

Conclusion: X-WAM成功实现了动作执行与高保真4D世界建模的统一，为未来智能机器人系统提供了高效且精确的环境理解与决策框架。

Abstract: We propose X-WAM, a Unified 4D World Model that unifies real-time robotic action execution and high-fidelity 4D world synthesis (video + 3D reconstruction) in a single framework, addressing the critical limitations of prior unified world models (e.g., UWM) that only model 2D pixel-space and fail to balance action efficiency and world modeling quality. To leverage the strong visual priors of pretrained video diffusion models, X-WAM imagines the future world by predicting multi-view RGB-D videos, and obtains spatial information efficiently through a lightweight structural adaptation: replicating the final few blocks of the pretrained Diffusion Transformer into a dedicated depth prediction branch for the reconstruction of future spatial information. Moreover, we propose Asynchronous Noise Sampling (ANS) to jointly optimize generation quality and action decoding efficiency. ANS applies a specialized asynchronous denoising schedule during inference, which rapidly decodes actions with fewer steps to enable efficient real-time execution, while dedicating the full sequence of steps to generate high-fidelity video. Rather than entirely decoupling the timesteps during training, ANS samples from their joint distribution to align with the inference distribution. Pretrained on over 5,800 hours of robotic data, X-WAM achieves 79.2% and 90.7% average success rate on RoboCasa and RoboTwin 2.0 benchmarks, while producing high-fidelity 4D reconstruction and generation surpassing existing methods in both visual and geometric metrics.

</details>


### [146] [Rule-based High-Level Coaching for Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning in Search-and-Rescue UAV Missions Under Limited-Simulation Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26833)
*Mahya Ramezani,Holger Voos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于无人机搜救任务的分层决策框架，结合了基于规则的高层顾问与在线目标条件强化学习控制器，在无预训练条件下提升了早期安全性与样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限模拟训练和无预训练部署条件下，提升无人机在搜索救援任务中的早期适应性、安全性和样本效率。

Method: 采用分层架构：高层为离线定义的规则化顾问，提供可解释的任务与安全指导；底层为在线目标条件强化学习控制器，结合模式感知的优先回放机制与规则衍生元数据进行经验复用。

Result: 在电池感知多目标投送和障碍环境中移动目标投送两项任务中，该方法显著减少碰撞终止，提高早期安全性和样本效率。

Conclusion: 所提框架能在严格无预训练条件下有效提升无人机任务的初期性能与安全性，同时保持对场景动态的在线适应能力。

Abstract: This paper presents a hierarchical decision-making framework for unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) missions motivated by search-and-rescue (SAR) scenarios under limited simulation training. The framework combines a fixed rule-based high-level advisor with an online goal-conditioned low-level reinforcement learning (RL) controller. To stress-test early adaptation, we also consider a strict no-pretraining deployment regime. The high-level advisor is defined offline from a structured task specification and compiled into deterministic rules. It provides interpretable mission- and safety-aware guidance through recommended actions, avoided actions, and regime-dependent arbitration weights. The low-level controller learns online from task-defined dense rewards and reuses experience through a mode-aware prioritized replay mechanism augmented with rule-derived metadata. We evaluate the framework on two tasks: battery-aware multi-goal delivery and moving-target delivery in obstacle-rich environments. Across both tasks, the proposed method improves early safety and sample efficiency primarily by reducing collision terminations, while preserving the ability to adapt online to scenario-specific dynamics.

</details>


### [147] [Walk With Me: Long-Horizon Social Navigation for Human-Centric Outdoor Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26839)
*Lingfeng Zhang,Xiaoshuai Hao,Xizhou Bu,Yingbo Tang,Hongsheng Li,Jinghui Lu,Xiu-shen Wei,Jiayi Ma,Yu Liu,Jing Zhang,Hangjun Ye,Xiaojun Liang,Long Chen,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需高精地图的长距离社交导航框架“Walk with Me”，通过自然语言指令实现安全、语义感知的户外机器人导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于地图的方法依赖昂贵的高精地图，且学习策略多限于室内和短程场景，难以满足开放户外环境中人类对机器人长期导航与社会合规性的需求。

Method: 利用GPS上下文和公共地图API的轻量级兴趣点进行语义目标定位与路径点提议；高层视觉-语言模型将抽象指令解析为具体目的地并规划粗略路径；在执行中，观测感知的路由机制决定由底层视觉-语言-动作策略处理常规路段，或触发高层模型进行安全推理以应对复杂情况。

Result: 实现了无需预建地图的长距离户外社交导航，在复杂场景（如拥挤路口）能主动停止等待，确保安全性与社会合规性。

Conclusion: Walk with Me 框架结合语义理解、无图长程规划、安全感知推理与低层动作生成，支持面向人类协助的实用化户外导航。

Abstract: Assisting humans in open-world outdoor environments requires robots to translate high-level natural-language intentions into safe, long-horizon, and socially compliant navigation behavior. Existing map-based methods rely on costly pre-built HD maps, while learning-based policies are mostly limited to indoor and short-horizon settings. To bridge this gap, we propose Walk with Me, a map-free framework for long-horizon social navigation from high-level human instructions. Walk with Me leverages GPS context and lightweight candidate points-of-interest from a public map API for semantic destination grounding and waypoint proposal. A High-Level Vision-Language Model grounds abstract instructions into concrete destinations and plans coarse waypoint sequences. During execution, an observation-aware routing mechanism determines whether the Low-Level Vision-Language-Action policy can handle the current situation or whether explicit safety reasoning from the High-Level VLM is needed. Routine segments are executed by the Low-Level VLA, while complex situations such as crowded crossings trigger high-level reasoning and stop-and-wait behavior when unsafe. By combining semantic intent grounding, map-free long-horizon planning, safety-aware reasoning, and low-level action generation, Walk with Me enables practical outdoor social navigation for human-centric assistance.

</details>


### [148] [STARRY: Spatial-Temporal Action-Centric World Modeling for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26848)
*Yuxuan Tian,Yurun Jin,Bin Yu,Yukun Shi,Hao Wu,Chi Harold Liu,Kai Chen,Cong Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: STARRY是一种增强的世界模型动作生成策略，通过联合去噪未来时空潜变量和动作序列，并引入几何感知的选择性注意力调制，实现了对机器人操作中时空交互的更好建模，在仿真和真实场景中均显著提升了任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略和基于世界模型的策略未能充分建模与动作相关的时空交互结构，限制了机器人在复杂空间-时间环境中的操作能力。

Method: 提出STARRY，联合去噪未来时空潜在表示与动作序列；引入几何感知的选择性注意力调制机制，将预测的深度和末端执行器几何信息转化为标记对齐的权重，用于选择性地调节动作注意力。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0上，STARRY在Clean和Randomized设置下分别达到93.82%和93.30%的平均成功率；在真实世界实验中，相比π₀.₅策略，平均成功率从42.5%提升至70.8%。

Conclusion: 以动作为中心的时空世界建模能有效提升对复杂时空交互任务的处理能力，验证了将预测与动作生成对齐的设计在机器人操作中的优越性。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation critically requires reasoning about future spatial-temporal interactions, yet existing VLA policies and world-model-enhanced policies do not fully model action-relevant spatial-temporal interaction structure. We propose STARRY, a world-model-enhanced action-generation policy that aligns spatial-temporal prediction with action generation. STARRY jointly denoises future spatial-temporal latents and action sequences, and introduces Geometry-Aware Selective Attention Modulation to convert predicted depth and end-effector geometry into token-aligned weights for selective action-attention modulation. On RoboTwin 2.0, STARRY achieves 93.82% / 93.30% average success under Clean and Randomized settings. Real-world experiments further improve average success from 42.5% to 70.8% over $π_{0.5}$, demonstrating the effectiveness of action-centric spatial-temporal world modeling for spatial-temporally demanding robotic action generation.

</details>


### [149] [Stochastic Entanglement of Deterministic Origami Tentacles For Universal Robotic Gripping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26897)
*Alec Boron,Bokun Zheng,Ziyang Zhou,Noel Naughton,Suyi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于折纸结构的肌腱驱动触手抓取器，通过局部确定性变形与全局随机缠绕的协同作用，实现对任意形状物体的稳定抓取，具有简单设计和驱动的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在动态工作环境中实现对任意形状物体的鲁棒抓取，同时避免增加驱动通道和控制复杂度。

Method: 设计了一种由薄Mylar片切割而成的折纸触手，通过精确布置孔位、折痕和锥形结构，在肌腱拉动下实现可控的收缩、弯曲和扭曲变形；多个触手靠近时可产生随机缠绕，形成编织或打结以抓取物体。结合折纸力学与Cosserat杆理论建立了仿真模型，并进行了实物实验验证。

Result: 成功实现了在重力环境、水下以及模拟轨道释放场景中的物体抓取；实验验证了该抓取器能够通过简单的肌腱驱动完成对不同形状物体的有效抓握。

Conclusion: 这种娱乐性折纸触手抓取器为简化设计和驱动条件下的鲁棒物体抓取提供了一种新策略。

Abstract: Origami-inspired robotic grippers have shown promising potential for object manipulation tasks due to their compact volume and mechanical flexibility. However, robust capture of objects with random shapes in dynamic working environments often comes at the cost of additional actuation channels and control complexity. Here, we introduce a tendon-driven origami tentacle gripper capable of universal object gripping by exploiting a synergy between local, deterministic deformation programming and global, stochastic entanglements. Each origami tentacle is made by cutting thin Mylar sheets; It features carefully placed holes for routing an actuation tendon, origami creases for controlling the deformation, and a tapered shape. By tailoring these design features, one can prescribe the shrinking, bending, and twisting deformation, eventually creating deterministic coiling with a simple tendon pull. Then, when multiple coiling tentacles are placed in proximity, stochastic entanglement emerges, allowing the tentacles to braid, knot, and grip objects with random shapes. We derived a simulation model by integrating origami mechanics with Cosserat rods to correlate origami design, tendon deformation, and their collective gripping performance. Then, we experimentally tested how these coiling and entangling origami tentacles can grasp objects under gravity and in water. A stow-and-release deployment mechanism was also tested to simulate in-orbit grasping. Overall, the entertaining origami tentacle gripper presents a new strategy for robust object grasping with simple design and actuation.

</details>


### [150] [Bi-Level Optimization for Contact and Motion Planning in Rope-Assisted Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26910)
*Ruben Malacarne,Ioannis Tsikelis,Enrico Mingo Hoffman,Michele Focchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于绳索辅助机器人在垂直表面攀爬的规划框架，采用双层优化方法解决混合整数问题，并在ALPINE机器人平台上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现绳索辅助机器人在复杂垂直地形上的高效与动态可行的运动规划，需要同时优化着陆区域选择和控制输入。

Method: 采用双层优化方案：外层使用交叉熵方法进行可行地形区域选择，内层使用基于梯度的非线性优化来计算动态可行的运动及控制输入（如绳索张力和腿部作用力）。

Result: 该方法在ALPINE攀爬机器人平台上进行了实验验证，能够应对多种复杂地形配置，实现了有效的运动规划。

Conclusion: 所提出的双层优化框架能够有效解决绳索辅助攀爬机器人的运动规划问题，兼顾地形选择与动态可行性，具有良好的应用潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents a planning pipeline framework for locomotion in rope-assisted robots climbing vertical surfaces. The proposed framework is formulated as a bi-level optimization scheme that addresses a mixed-integer problem: selecting feasible terrain regions for landing while simultaneously optimizing the control inputs, namely rope tensions and leg forces, and landing location. The outer level of the optimization is solved using the Cross-Entropy Method, while the inner level relies on gradient-based nonlinear optimization to compute dynamically feasible motions. The approach is validated on a novel climbing robot platform, ALPINE, across a variety of challenging terrain configurations.

</details>
